				1941 = { gp_white_lion_hunting_grounds_modifier_trigger = yes }
				1941 = {
					has_great_project = {
						type = gp_white_lion_hunting_grounds
						tier = 1
					}
					NOT = { has_great_project = {
						type = gp_white_lion_hunting_grounds
						tier = 2
					}}
					NOT = { has_great_project = {
						type = gp_white_lion_hunting_grounds
						tier = 3
					}}
					owner = {
						is_subject = yes
						NOT = { is_subject_of_type = tributary_state }
					}
				}
			}
			1941 = { 
				owner = { 
					overlord = {
						add_country_modifier = {
							name = gp_white_lion_hunting_grounds1
							duration = -1
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				1941 = { gp_white_lion_hunting_grounds_modifier_trigger = yes }
				1941 = {
					has_great_project = {
						type = gp_white_lion_hunting_grounds

					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					1941 = { gp_white_lion_hunting_grounds_modifier_trigger = no }
					1941 = {
						owner = {
							has_country_flag = dependencylost
						}
					}
				}
			}
			every_country = {
				remove_country_modifier = gp_white_lion_hunting_grounds1
				remove_country_modifier = gp_white_lion_hunting_grounds2
				remove_country_modifier = gp_white_lion_hunting_grounds3
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = subjectmonumentlost
				OR ={
					has_country_modifier = gp_white_lion_hunting_grounds1
					has_country_modifier = gp_white_lion_hunting_grounds2
					has_country_modifier = gp_white_lion_hunting_grounds3
				}
			}
			remove_country_modifier = gp_white_lion_hunting_grounds1
			remove_country_modifier = gp_white_lion_hunting_grounds2
			remove_country_modifier = gp_white_lion_hunting_grounds3
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				1967 = { gp_great_vortex_modifier_trigger = yes }
				1967 = {
					has_great_project = {
						type = gp_great_vortex
						tier = 1

						type = gp_great_vortex_corrupted
						tier = 3
					}
					owner = {

			1967 = {

							duration = -1
						}

					has_country_modifier = gp_great_vortex_corrupted2
